Trace summary
0.310 final reward

Submission

The agent submitted a looped transformer that repeats a pair of width-512 blocks 16 times, for 32 block applications. Each block has four attention heads and a GELU feed-forward layer. Each application has its own normalization and feature scales, mixes features from previous token positions, and learns how to combine hidden states from earlier passes.

Training used AdamW with 5% warmup and cosine decay. The final weights came from an exponential moving average that started halfway through training.

  • /workspace/submission/train.py (3.1 KB)

Scientific process

  • Compare how to spend the model budget. Repeating the starter block design improved dev loss. The agent then varied width, depth, and which weights to share. Muon and several partial weight-sharing designs did worse than the wider blocks trained with AdamW. For the two-block model repeated eight times, averaging weights during training lowered mean dev loss from 3.544 to 3.531 across two seeds. Weight averaging results
  • Separate token mixing from depth conditioning. The agent tested mixing features across nearby token positions separately from gates and skip connections that vary by pass. Token mixing alone barely helped. Removing it from the model with depth conditioning kept most of the gain. Ablation results
  • Compare the two finalists. The agent compared one block applied 16 times with two alternating blocks applied 32 times in total. Dev loss averaged 3.453 for the deeper recipe and 3.461 for the validated single-block recipe, each over three seeds. The final seed trained from the cleaned submission files. Final verification

"""The starting model: a small dense GPT-2-style language model.
Four transformer blocks (width 256, 4 heads, causal attention with RoPE on
queries and keys, GELU-tanh MLP of width 896), each used once per token. The
model returns the final hidden states; the harness turns them into logits
with the token embedding table (tied output head). 2,896,928 unique weights besides
the 8192 x 256 embedding (not counted), about 12M FLOPs per token.
Initialisation: weights N(0, 0.02), biases 0, and every block's output
projections (attention and MLP c_proj) N(0, 0.02 / sqrt(2 * n_layers)).
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
from dataclasses import dataclass
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn
@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 256
    n_heads: int = 4
    n_layers: int = 4
    mlp_width: int = 896
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-5
class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        self.c_attn = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, 3 * cfg.d_model)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)
    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        batch, length, width = x.shape
        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(width, dim=2)
        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, width))
class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.c_fc = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.mlp_width)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, cfg.d_model)
    def forward(self, x):
        return self.c_proj(F.gelu(self.c_fc(x), approximate="tanh"))
class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln_1 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.attn = Attention(cfg)
        self.ln_2 = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)
    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)
        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))
def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)
class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
        head_dim = cfg.d_model // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.blocks.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers))
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version
    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):
        for block in self.blocks:
            h = block(h, cos, sin)
        return h
    def forward(self, input_ids):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin())
        return self.ln_f(h)  # the harness applies the tied output head
def build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""
    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
"""Starting training recipe: plain next-token loss.
Random 512-token windows, AdamW (betas 0.9, 0.95; weight decay on matrices
only), bf16 autocast, gradient clipping at 1, a linear warmup over the first
5% of the budget and a cosine decay to 10% of the learning rate over the
rest. Training stops when the budget is spent.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
def train(ctx):
    cfg = ctx.config["train"]
    model, budget = ctx.model, ctx.budget
    if cfg["compile"] and ctx.device.type == "cuda":
        model.inner.body.stack = torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params = list(model.parameters())
    optimizer = torch.optim.AdamW([{"params": [p for p in params if p.dim() >= 2], "weight_decay": cfg["weight_decay"]},
                                   {"params": [p for p in params if p.dim() < 2], "weight_decay": 0.0}],
                                  lr=cfg["learning_rate"], betas=(0.9, 0.95), fused=ctx.device.type == "cuda")
    def lr_at(progress: float) -> float:
        lr, warmup = cfg["learning_rate"], cfg["warmup"]
        if progress < warmup:
            return lr * max(progress, 1e-4) / warmup
        p = (progress - warmup) / (1 - warmup)
        return lr * (0.1 + 0.9 * 0.5 * (1 + math.cos(math.pi * min(1.0, p))))
    update = 0
    model.train()
    while not budget.exhausted:
        for group in optimizer.param_groups:
            group["lr"] = lr_at(budget.fraction_used)
        total = 0.0
        for _ in range(cfg["accumulation"]):
            batch = ctx.data.sample(cfg["micro_batch"])
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16):
                logits = model(batch.tokens)
            loss = F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), batch.targets.flatten())
            (loss / cfg["accumulation"]).backward()
            total += loss.item() / cfg["accumulation"]
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params, 1.0)
        optimizer.step()
        optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update += 1
        if not math.isfinite(total):
            raise FloatingPointError(f"loss is {total} at update {update}")
        if update % cfg["log_every"] == 0:
            ctx.log({"update": update, "loss": total, "lr": optimizer.param_groups[0]["lr"]})
"""What a submission provides.
submission/model.py defines
    def build_model(config: dict) -> nn.Module
where config is submission/config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...}. The model
maps token ids to hidden states, and must have its token embedding table as
the attribute embed = nn.Embedding(vocab_size, width):
    hidden = model(input_ids)              # [B, L] -> [B, L, width]
The harness owns the output layer: it wraps the model in
harness.OutputHead, which computes the next-token logits as
    logits = hidden @ model.embed.weight.T  # [B, L, vocab]
so every prediction goes through that table. Everything below calls the
wrapped model, which returns logits. Position i's logits may depend only on
tokens 0..i. Evaluation calls
model(input_ids) in eval mode with no other arguments; that call must stay
within task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP FLOPs per token. Any architecture is allowed
(dense, shared or looped blocks, anything else) within task.WEIGHT_CAP
unique weights.
In training, train.py may pass keyword arguments (say, a number of loops):
model(input_ids, loops=12). How much compute a call costs may depend only on
those arguments, the training/eval mode and the input's shape, never on
random draws inside the model or on the batch size: the harness measures
each kind of call once and charges every later call of that kind its tokens
times that cost.
submission/train.py defines
    def train(ctx: TrainContext) -> None
which trains ctx.model (a metered wrapper around OutputHead(build_model(config)),
see harness.MeteredModel; ctx.model(input_ids) returns logits, and the
submission's own module is ctx.model.inner.body) until the budget runs out: task.TRAIN_TOKENS tokens
through the model and task.TRAIN_FLOPS forward FLOPs, whichever comes first.
A call after that raises BudgetExhausted, which ends training normally. The
harness saves the model's weights when train returns or the budget is spent.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any, Callable
import torch
class BudgetExhausted(RuntimeError):
    """Raised by ctx.model when it is called after the training budget is spent; ends training normally."""
@dataclass
class Batch:
    tokens: torch.Tensor  # [B, L] input token ids
    targets: torch.Tensor  # [B, L] the next token at each position
@dataclass
class TrainContext:
    model: Any  # harness.MeteredModel
    budget: Any  # harness.Budget: .exhausted, .fraction_used, .used_tokens, .used_flops
    data: Any  # harness.TokenStream: .sample(batch_size, seq_len=task.SEQ_LEN) -> Batch
    config: dict  # submission/config.json
    seed: int
    device: torch.device
    log: Callable[[dict], None]  # records a dict of numbers in the run's train.json (and prints it)
"""The harness around a submission: weight count, FLOP metering, data, training run, evaluation.
Compute is counted in FLOPs with torch's FlopCounterMode (uncompiled; on the
CPU attention is swapped for an explicit matmul version so it is counted).
The submission's model returns hidden states; build() wraps it in
OutputHead, which turns them into logits with the model's token embedding
table, so every model here maps ids to logits. train.py sees the model only
through MeteredModel. Each kind of call (mode,
input length, keyword arguments) is measured once on a one-row batch, with
autograd on as in training; every call then charges its tokens and tokens x
that cost to the budget. A call after the budget is spent raises
BudgetExhausted, which ends training normally. These checks run inside the
submission's process and catch honest mistakes; the grader re-checks the
model from outside (checks.py).
"""
from __future__ import annotations
import contextlib
import copy
import importlib.util
import json
import math
import sys
import time
import traceback
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
from torch import nn
from torch.utils.flop_counter import FlopCounterMode
from lmenv import task
from lmenv.api import Batch, BudgetExhausted, TrainContext
@dataclass
class Budget:
    tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS
    flops: float = task.TRAIN_FLOPS
    used_tokens: float = 0.0
    used_flops: float = 0.0
    started: float = field(default_factory=time.monotonic)
    def charge(self, tokens: float, flops: float) -> None:
        if self.exhausted:
            raise BudgetExhausted(self.describe())
        self.used_tokens += float(tokens)
        self.used_flops += float(flops)
    @property
    def elapsed_s(self) -> float:
        return time.monotonic() - self.started
    @property
    def exhausted(self) -> bool:
        return self.fraction_used >= 1.0
    @property
    def fraction_used(self) -> float:
        return max(self.used_tokens / self.tokens, self.used_flops / self.flops)
    def describe(self) -> str:
        return (f"{self.used_tokens:.3g}/{self.tokens:.3g} tokens, {self.used_flops:.3g}/{self.flops:.3g} FLOPs, "
                f"{self.elapsed_s:.0f} s")
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- measurements
@contextlib.contextmanager
def countable_attention(device: torch.device):
    """On the CPU, FlopCounterMode misses fused attention: count an explicit version instead."""
    if device.type != "cpu":
        yield
        return
    original = F.scaled_dot_product_attention
    def explicit(q, k, v, attn_mask=None, dropout_p=0.0, is_causal=False, scale=None, **_):
        scores = q @ k.transpose(-2, -1) * (scale or 1.0 / math.sqrt(q.shape[-1]))
        if is_causal:
            length = q.shape[-2]
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~torch.ones(length, length, dtype=torch.bool).tril(), float("-inf"))
        if attn_mask is not None:
            scores = scores.masked_fill(~attn_mask, float("-inf")) if attn_mask.dtype == torch.bool else scores + attn_mask
        return torch.softmax(scores, dim=-1) @ v
    F.scaled_dot_product_attention = explicit
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        F.scaled_dot_product_attention = original
def flops_per_token(model: nn.Module, ids: torch.Tensor, kwargs: dict | None = None, training: bool = False,
                    grad: bool = False) -> float:
    """Forward FLOPs per token of model(ids, **kwargs) in the given mode, uncompiled."""
    was_training = model.training
    model.train(training)
    devices = [ids.device] if ids.device.type == "cuda" else []
    # force_eager runs compiled functions uncompiled just for this call. (Setting
    # torch._dynamo.config.disable instead marks a compiled function that is first
    # called here as never to be compiled, so training ran uncompiled.)
    try:
        with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=devices), torch.set_grad_enabled(grad), countable_attention(ids.device), \
                torch.compiler.set_stance("force_eager"):
            with FlopCounterMode(display=False) as counter:
                model(ids, **(kwargs or {}))
        return counter.get_total_flops() / ids.numel()
    finally:
        model.train(was_training)
def unique_weights(model: nn.Module, vocab_size: int) -> dict:
    """Unique floating-point weights (parameters and buffers, one count per storage), minus one exempt table.
    The exempt table is the largest stored tensor of shape (vocab, width) with
    width <= EXEMPT_TABLE_WIDTH: the token embedding, or the head tied to it.
    Integer and boolean buffers (masks, indices) are not weights and are not counted.
    """
    storages: dict[int, tuple[int, tuple]] = {}
    for tensor in [*model.parameters(), *model.buffers()]:
        if not (tensor.is_floating_point() or tensor.is_complex()) or tensor.numel() == 0:
            continue
        storage = tensor.untyped_storage()
        elements = storage.nbytes() // tensor.element_size()
        key = storage.data_ptr()
        if key not in storages or storages[key][0] < elements:
            storages[key] = (elements, tuple(tensor.shape))
    total = sum(n for n, _ in storages.values())
    tables = [n for n, shape in storages.values()
              if len(shape) == 2 and shape[0] == vocab_size and shape[1] <= task.EXEMPT_TABLE_WIDTH and n == shape[0] * shape[1]]
    exempt = max(tables, default=0)
    return {"total": total, "exempt": exempt, "counted": total - exempt}
@torch.no_grad()
def interface_failures(model: nn.Module, vocab_size: int, device, perturb: bool = True) -> list[str]:
    """model(ids) gives [B, L, vocab] logits, and position i depends only on tokens 0..i.
    With ``perturb``, the test runs on a copy with small noise added to every
    weight: a freshly initialised model can hide a leak behind zero-initialised
    projections or gates that training would open.
    """
    if perturb:
        model = copy.deepcopy(model)
        noise = torch.Generator(device="cpu").manual_seed(1)
        for p in model.parameters():
            if p.is_floating_point():
                p.add_(0.02 * torch.randn(p.shape, generator=noise).to(p.device, p.dtype))
    was_training = model.training
    model.eval()
    gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)
    ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, 64), generator=gen).to(device)
    changed = ids.clone()
    changed[:, 40:] = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (2, 24), generator=gen).to(device)
    try:
        logits = model(ids).float()
        if tuple(logits.shape) != (2, 64, vocab_size):
            return [f"the model's output has shape {tuple(logits.shape)}, expected (batch, length, vocab)"]
        moved = (model(changed).float()[:, :40] - logits[:, :40]).abs().max().item()
        if moved > 1e-3:
            return [f"not causal: changing tokens 40.. moves the logits of earlier positions by {moved:.3g}"]
    except Exception as error:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        return [f"the model cannot be run: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:500]]
    finally:
        model.train(was_training)
    return []
def cap_failures(model: nn.Module, vocab_size: int, device) -> tuple[dict, list[str]]:
    """Unique weights and inference FLOPs per token against the caps."""
    weights = unique_weights(model, vocab_size)
    ids = torch.randint(0, vocab_size, (1, task.SEQ_LEN), generator=torch.Generator().manual_seed(0)).to(device)
    flops = flops_per_token(model, ids)
    failures = []
    if weights["counted"] > task.WEIGHT_CAP:
        failures.append(f"{weights['counted']:,} unique weights (cap {task.WEIGHT_CAP:,}; one embedding table of "
                        f"{weights['exempt']:,} not counted)")
    if flops > task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP:
        failures.append(f"inference costs {flops / 1e6:.1f}M FLOPs per token (cap {task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP / 1e6:.0f}M)")
    return {"weights": weights, "inference_flops_per_token": flops}, failures
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- the metered model
def _call_key(training: bool, ids: torch.Tensor, kwargs: dict) -> tuple:
    for name, value in kwargs.items():
        if not isinstance(value, (bool, int, float, str, type(None))):
            raise TypeError(f"model keyword argument {name!r} must be a number, string, bool or None, "
                            f"not {type(value).__name__}")
    return (training, ids.dim(), ids.shape[-1], tuple(sorted(kwargs.items())))
class MeteredModel(nn.Module):
    """ctx.model: calls the submission's model and charges each call to the budget."""
    def __init__(self, model: nn.Module, budget: Budget):
        super().__init__()
        self.inner = model
        self.budget = budget
        self.costs: dict[tuple, float] = {}
    def forward(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor, **kwargs) -> torch.Tensor:
        if self.budget.exhausted:
            raise BudgetExhausted(self.budget.describe())
        key = _call_key(self.inner.training, input_ids, kwargs)
        if key not in self.costs:
            if len(self.costs) >= task.MAX_CALL_KINDS:
                raise RuntimeError(f"more than {task.MAX_CALL_KINDS} kinds of model call")
            self.costs[key] = flops_per_token(self.inner, input_ids[:1], kwargs, training=self.inner.training,
                                              grad=torch.is_grad_enabled())
        tokens = input_ids.numel()
        self.budget.charge(tokens, tokens * self.costs[key])
        return self.inner(input_ids, **kwargs)
    def cost_table(self) -> list[dict]:
        return [{"training": k[0], "length": k[2], "kwargs": dict(k[3]), "flops_per_token": v}
                for k, v in self.costs.items()]
class TokenStream:
    """One pass over the first ``corpus`` training tokens (CORPUS_TOKENS by default), in shuffled order.
    The text is cut into blocks of SEQ_LEN tokens, visited in a random order;
    every token is a target once per pass. A row of seq_len < SEQ_LEN takes the
    next seq_len tokens of the current block (the block's remainder shorter than
    seq_len is skipped). When the blocks run out, a new pass starts in a new order.
    """
    def __init__(self, data: Path, seed: int, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS):
        self.tokens = np.memmap(data / "train.bin", dtype=np.uint16, mode="r")[:int(corpus)]
        self.rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
        self.device = torch.device("cpu")
        self.blocks = (len(self.tokens) - 1) // task.SEQ_LEN
        self.order, self.next_block, self.offset = self.rng.permutation(self.blocks), 0, task.SEQ_LEN
        self.passes = 0
    def _row_start(self, seq_len: int) -> int:
        if self.offset + seq_len > task.SEQ_LEN:
            if self.next_block == self.blocks:
                self.order, self.next_block = self.rng.permutation(self.blocks), 0
                self.passes += 1
            self.block, self.next_block, self.offset = self.order[self.next_block], self.next_block + 1, 0
        start = int(self.block) * task.SEQ_LEN + self.offset
        self.offset += seq_len
        return start
    def sample(self, batch_size: int, seq_len: int = task.SEQ_LEN) -> Batch:
        if not 1 <= seq_len <= task.SEQ_LEN:
            raise ValueError(f"seq_len must be in 1..{task.SEQ_LEN}")
        starts = [self._row_start(seq_len) for _ in range(batch_size)]
        rows = np.stack([self.tokens[s:s + seq_len + 1] for s in starts]).astype(np.int64)
        rows = torch.from_numpy(rows).to(self.device, non_blocking=True)
        return Batch(tokens=rows[:, :-1], targets=rows[:, 1:])
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- submission code
def load_module(directory: Path, name: str):
    path = directory / f"{name}.py"
    if not path.exists():
        return None
    if str(directory.resolve()) not in sys.path:
        sys.path.insert(0, str(directory.resolve()))
    spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(name, path)
    module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
    sys.modules[name] = module  # so train.py can "import model"
    spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return module
def read_config(submission: Path) -> dict:
    path = submission / "config.json"
    return json.loads(path.read_text()) if path.exists() else {}
class OutputHead(nn.Module):
    """The submission's model followed by the harness's output layer.
    The submission's model (``body``) returns hidden states [B, L, width]; the
    logits are those states times its token embedding table, body.embed
    (vocab x width): logits = hidden @ body.embed.weight.T. Every prediction
    therefore goes through the tied table, and nothing can put probability on
    tokens any other way (for example by copying ids from the input).
    """
    def __init__(self, body: nn.Module, vocab_size: int):
        super().__init__()
        embed = getattr(body, "embed", None)
        if not isinstance(embed, nn.Embedding) or embed.num_embeddings != vocab_size:
            raise TypeError(f"the model needs an attribute embed = nn.Embedding({vocab_size}, width); "
                            f"the harness computes the logits as hidden @ embed.weight.T")
        self.body = body
    def forward(self, input_ids: torch.Tensor, **kwargs) -> torch.Tensor:
        hidden = self.body(input_ids, **kwargs)
        if not isinstance(hidden, torch.Tensor):
            raise TypeError(f"the model must return hidden states [batch, length, width], not {type(hidden).__name__}")
        return F.linear(hidden, self.body.embed.weight)
def build(submission: Path, vocab_size: int, device) -> nn.Module:
    """The submission's model inside the harness's output layer (OutputHead)."""
    module = load_module(submission, "model")
    if module is None:
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"{submission}/model.py is missing")
    body = module.build_model({**read_config(submission), "vocab_size": vocab_size})
    retu

    model.load_state_dict(weights)
    return model.eval()
def saved_state(model: nn.Module) -> dict:
    # torch.compile'd submodules add "_orig_mod." to their names; save the plain names.
    return {k.replace("_orig_mod.", ""): v.detach().cpu() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}
# ---------------------------------------------------------------- training and evaluation
def train_run(submission: Path, data: Path, out: Path, seed: int, device, tokens: float = task.TRAIN_TOKENS,
              log=print, corpus: int = task.CORPUS_TOKENS) -> dict:
    """Train one seed of the submission; writes OUT/model.pt and OUT/train.json and returns the record.
    ``tokens`` below TRAIN_TOKENS scales both budgets down (a quick proxy run).
    ``corpus`` (design experiments only) sets how many training tokens are sampled from.
    """
    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    vocab = json.loads((data / "meta.json").read_text())["vocab_size"]
    record: dict = {"seed": seed, "status": "error", "error": None, "budget_tokens": tokens, "corpus": corpus,
                    "history": []}
    def finish(**fields) -> dict:
        record.update(fields)
        (out / "train.json").write_text(json.dumps(record, indent=2))
        return record
    try:
        torch.manual_seed(seed)
        np.random.seed(seed)
        model = build(submission, vocab, device)
        trainer = load_module(submission, "train")
        if trainer is None:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"{submission}/train.py is missing")
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"building the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    failures = interface_failures(model, vocab, device)
    if failures:
        return finish(error=failures[0])
    try:
        caps, failures = cap_failures(model, vocab, device)
    except Exception as error:
        return finish(error=f"measuring the model: {type(error).__name__}: {error}"[:2000])
    log(f"{caps['weights']['counted']:,} unique weights (+{caps['weights']['exempt']:,} embedding), "
        f"{caps['inference_flops_per_token'] / 1e6:.1f}M inference FLOPs per token")
    if failures:
        return finish(error="over the caps: " + "; ".join(failures), caps=caps)
    meter = Budget(tokens=tokens, flops=tokens * task.INFERENCE_FLOP_CAP)
    metered = MeteredModel(model, meter)
    stream = TokenStream(data, seed, corpus)
    stream.device = device
    history = record["history"]
    def record_log(entry: dict) -> None:
        entry = {**entry, "tokens": meter.used_tokens, "flops": meter.used_flops, "elapsed_s": round(meter.elapsed_s, 1)}
        history.append(entry)
        log("  ".join(f"{k} {v:.4g}" if isinstance(v, float) else f"{k} {v}" for k, v in entry.items()))
    ctx = TrainContext(model=metered, budget=meter, data=stream, config=read_config(submission), seed=seed,
                       device=device, log=record_log)
    status, error = "complete", None
    started = time.time()
    try:
        trainer.train(ctx)
    except BudgetExhausted:
        pass
    except Exception as e:  # the submission's code can fail in any way
        status, error = "error", f"train.py: {type(e).__name__}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()[-1500:]}"
    torch.save(saved_state(model), out / "model.pt")
    return finish(status=status, error=error, caps=caps, used_tokens=meter.used_tokens, used_flops=meter.used_flops,
                  call_costs=metered.cost_table(), train_seconds=time.time() - started,
                  corpus_passes=stream.passes + stream.next_block / stream.blocks)
_TOKEN_BYTES: dict = {}
def token_bytes(tokenizer: Path, eot: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """UTF-8 bytes of each token of our byte-level BPE (the end-of-text token counts as one byte)."""
    key = str(tokenizer)
    if key not in _TOKEN_BYTES:
        vocab = json.loads(tokenizer.read_text())["model"]["vocab"]
        # GPT-2's byte-to-character map: printable bytes stand for themselves, the rest from 256 up.
        printable = [*range(ord("!"), ord("~") + 1), *range(ord("¡"), ord("¬") + 1), *range(ord("®"), ord("ÿ") + 1)]
        to_char = {b: chr(b) for b in printable}
        extra = 0
        for b in range(256):
            if b not in to_char:
                to_char[b] = chr(256 + extra)
                extra += 1
        known = set(to_char.values())
        lengths = np.zeros(max(vocab.values()) + 1, dtype=np.int64)
        for piece, index in vocab.items():
            lengths[index] = len(piece) if all(c in known for c in piece) else len(piece.encode())
        lengths[eot] = 1
        _TOKEN_BYTES[key] = lengths
    return _TOKEN_BYTES[key]
@torch.no_grad()
def evaluate(model: nn.Module, tokens: np.ndarray, device, byte_lengths: np.ndarray | None = None,
             batch: int = 32) -> dict:
    """Mean next-token loss (nats per token) on every full SEQ_LEN window of ``tokens``, and bits per byte."""
    model.eval()
    n = (len(tokens) - 1) // task.SEQ_LEN
    total = torch.zeros((), dtype=torch.float64, device=device)
    torch.manual_seed(0)
    for start in range(0, n, batch):
        stop = min(n, start + batch)
        rows = np.stack([tokens[i * task.SEQ_LEN:(i + 1) * task.SEQ_LEN + 1] for i in range(start, stop)])
        rows = torch.from_numpy(rows.astype(np.int64)).to(device)
        with torch.autocast(device.type, dtype=torch.bfloat16, enabled=device.type == "cuda"):
            logits = model(rows[:, :-1])
        total += F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0, 1), rows[:, 1:].flatten(), reduction="sum")
    loss = total.item() / (n * task.SEQ_LEN)
    result = {"loss": loss if math.isfinite(loss) else float("inf"), "tokens": n * task.SEQ_LEN}
    if byte_lengths is not None:
        targets = np.asarray(tokens[1:n * task.SEQ_LEN + 1], dtype=np.int64)
        nbytes = int(byte_lengths[targets].sum())
        result["bits_per_byte"] = total.item() / math.log(2) / nbytes
        result["bytes"] = nbytes
    return result

report.failures.extend(failures)\n        check_eval(model, ids, text, report)\n        check_call_costs(model, recorded_calls or [], vocab_size, device, report)\n    except Exception as error:\n We want to build a language model that only has few fixed weights but may spend plenty of compute on every token. Your goal is to build the best such model you can, through systematic research and experimentation.
## Setup
You get a small transformer language model with about 2.9 million weights (plus its token embedding), trained on web text (FineWeb-Edu) with plain next-token prediction loss. It uses about 12 million FLOPs per token, far less than the cap below allows.
You may change the model and the whole training procedure: the architecture inside the caps below, the losses, the optimiser, the schedule, and so on. The training data, the number of training tokens and the caps are fixed.
## Objective
Minimise the loss (cross-entropy of the next token, in nats) of your trained model on held-out text.
We will retrain your method from scratch once on each of two texts of our own and test each model on held-out text of the similar kind. Your score would the average over both of our held out datasets. Lower loss is better. We will compare your loss with the starting point's and with reference models of our own.
On your dev text, the starting point has a loss of about 3.64 (1.351 bits per byte; see `data/lm/reference.json`).
## The model interface and the caps
`model.py` defines `build_model(config)`. Your model returns hidden states, `hidden = model(input_ids, **options)`, and must keep its token embedding table as `embed` (an `nn.Embedding` of shape (8192, width)). You cannot touch the output layer (it is controlled by the harness): the next-token logits are `hidden @ embed.weight.T`, so every prediction goes through that table. Options you pass in training are up to you and we evaluate with `model(input_ids)` in eval mode. The full interface is documented in `/opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/api.py`.
- Weights: at most 3 million unique weights. Every floating-point parameter and buffer counts once, however often it is used. One table of shape (8192, width) with width at most 512 (the token embedding, or an output head tied to it) does not count.
- Inference compute: at most 120 million FLOPs per token for `model(input_ids)` on a 512-token window, measured with PyTorch's FLOP counter.
- Position i's logits may depend only on tokens 0..i, and evaluation must be deterministic and use no random numbers.
`python -m lmenv.run check` runs these checks.
## Training budget
Training gets 250 million tokens: every token passed through the model in training counts, one pass over the first 250 million tokens of the training text (`ctx.data.sample` returns windows of up to 512 tokens in a shuffled order, each token once). The forward FLOPs of all training calls together may average at most 120 million per token. Backward passes are not counted. The harness measures the cost of each kind of call to the model once and charges every call its tokens and FLOPs. Training stops when either budget is spent.
All computation with trainable weights must happen inside calls to the metered model (`ctx.model`). Losses computed from the logits are free.
## What you have
- `submission/`: the starting point. Whatever is in this folder at the end is your submission.
  - `model.py`: the model.
  - `train.py`: `train(ctx)`, the training loop. It gets the metered model, the budget, the data stream and the config. See `ctx` in `/opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/api.py` and the harness in `/opt/lmenv-src/lmenv/harness.py`.
  - `config.json`: passed to both.
- `data/lm/`: dev training and validation text, already tokenized (`train.bin`, `val.bin`, `tokenizer.json`) and `reference.json` (the starting point's dev numbers).
- The `lmenv` package, which runs everything (run from `/workspace`):
  - `python -m lmenv.run check` runs the checks on the model.
  - `python -m lmenv.run train --out runs/NAME --seed N` trains one seed with the same budget and rules we use, then prints the validation loss. `--tokens` sets a smaller budget for quick tests.
  - `python -m lmenv.run eval --run runs/NAME` evaluates a trained run again.
  - `python -m lmenv.run score --runs runs/A runs/B runs/C` averages several runs the way we do and shows how much lower the loss is than the starting point's.
- `timer.sh`: tells you how much time you have left.
- `runs/` for your runs and `snapshots/` for saved versions of your method (see below).
## Running experiments
- One training run of the starting point takes about 2 minutes. Start long jobs in the background, for example `nohup python -m lmenv.run train ... > runs/NAME.out 2>&1 &`, so you can keep working.
- You can run more than one job on the GPU at the same time.
- Results vary between seeds by about a hundredth of a nat. Compare methods over several seeds before trusting a small difference.
## Saving versions of your method
Whenever you have a version you consider an improvement, or before you change direction, save it:
    python -m lmenv.run snapshot --note "what you changed and what you saw" --runs runs/NAME ...
This copies `submission/` into `snapshots/` with your note. We may retrain your snapshots afterwards to see how your method developed. They do not affect your score. Your final score comes from what is in `submission/` at the end.
## Information on the setup
- The machine has one NVIDIA H200 141GB GPU, 16 CPU cores and PyTorch 2.7.1.
- There is no internet access: the commands you run cannot reach the network, and no web search is available.
## Rules
1. There will be no user interaction. You have to operate autonomously.
2. You have a maximum of 4 hours. You can check how much time you have left by running `bash timer.sh`.
3. We retrain your submission with the same harness (`lmenv.harness.train_run`). It must keep the interface of the starting point: `model.py` with `build_model(config)`, `train.py` with `train(ctx)`, and `config.json`.
4. Use the model only through `ctx.model`, and do not work around the budget or the caps (for example, by computing with trainable weights outside the model call, or by making a call's cost depend on the batch size).
5. No distillation and no pretrained weights: the model learns only from the training tokens it is given.
6. Your submission may read only the training tokens it is given and its own files. It must not read the validation text, access the network or other processes, or touch files outside `submission/`.
7. Your submission must run in the current environment (same packages). New packages cannot be installed.
8. You must NOT use any API key or login found in your environment to call an external LLM API directly. They are there to run you (the agent), not for you to query other models.
Submissions that break these rules receive the starting point's score.
Remember: NEVER ask the user for feedback. Just execute actions which make most sense to you. We will retrain and evaluate your submission once you are done.

from pathlib import Path
p=Path('submission/model.py')
s=p.read_text().replace('    mlp_width: int = 896','    mlp_width: int = 896\n    repeats: int = 3\n    depth_norm: bool = True\n    init_scale: float = 1.0')
s=s.replace('    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):\n        x = x + self.attn(self.ln_1(x), cos, sin)\n        return x + self.mlp(self.ln_2(x))','    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, norms=None):\n        ln_1, ln_2 = (self.ln_1, self.ln_2) if norms is None else norms\n        x = x + self.attn(ln_1(x), cos, sin)\n        return x + self.mlp(ln_2(x))')
s=s.replace('        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm', '        self.depth_norms = nn.ModuleList(nn.ModuleList([nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps), nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)]) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats)) if cfg.depth_norm else None\n        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm')
s=s.replace('math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers)', 'math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats) / cfg.init_scale')
s=s.replace('        for block in self.blocks:\n            h = block(h, cos, sin)\n        return h','        for r in range(self.cfg.repeats):\n            for i, block in enumerate(self.blocks):\n                norms = self.depth_norms[r * self.cfg.n_layers + i] if self.depth_norms is not None else None\n                h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)\n        return h')
p.write_text(s)

from pathlib import Path

"""Token-budgeted next-token training with AdamW or Muon."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

@torch.compile
def orthogonalize(g, steps=5):
    x = g.bfloat16()
    transpose = x.shape[0] > x.shape[1]
    if transpose:
        x = x.T
    x = x / (x.norm() + 1e-7)
    for _ in range(steps):
        a = x @ x.T
        b = -4.7750 * a + 2.0315 * (a @ a)
        x = 3.4445 * x + b @ x
    return x.T if transpose else x

class Muon(torch.optim.Optimizer):
    def __init__(self, params, lr, weight_decay=0.0, momentum=0.95):
        super().__init__(params, dict(lr=lr, weight_decay=weight_decay, momentum=momentum))
    @torch.no_grad()
    def step(self):
        for group in self.param_groups:
            for p in group['params']:
                if p.grad is None:
                    continue
                state = self.state[p]
                if 'momentum' not in state:
                    state['momentum'] = torch.zeros_like(p)
                buf=state['momentum']
                buf.lerp_(p.grad, 1-group['momentum'])
                g=p.grad.lerp(buf, group['momentum'])
                update=orthogonalize(g)
                p.mul_(1-group['lr']*group['weight_decay'])
                p.add_(update, alpha=-group['lr']*max(1,p.shape[0]/p.shape[1])**0.5)

@torch.compile
def token_loss(logits, targets):
    return F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1), targets.flatten())

def train(ctx):
    cfg=ctx.config['train']; model=ctx.model; budget=ctx.budget
    if cfg.get('compile',True) and ctx.device.type=='cuda':
        model.inner.body.stack=torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params=list(model.parameters())
    if cfg.get('optimizer','adamw')=='muon':
        matrix=[]; other=[]
        for name,p in model.named_parameters():
            (matrix if p.ndim==2 and 'embed' not in name else other).append(p)
        optimizers=[Muon(matrix, cfg.get('muon_lr',0.02),cfg.get('muon_decay',0.1)),torch.optim.AdamW(other,lr=cfg['learning_rate'],betas=(0.9,cfg.get('beta2',0.95)),weight_decay=cfg.get('embed_decay',0.1),fused=True)]
    else:
        groups=[{'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if p.dim()>=2 and 'embed' not in n], 'weight_decay':cfg['weight_decay']},
                {'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if 'embed' in n], 'weight_decay':cfg.get('embed_decay',cfg['weight_decay']), 'lr':cfg['learning_rate']*cfg.get('embed_lr_mult',1.0)},
                {'params':[p for p in params if p.dim()<2], 'weight_decay':0.0}]
        optimizers=[torch.optim.AdamW(groups,lr=cfg['learning_rate'],betas=(0.9,cfg.get('beta2',0.95)),fused=ctx.device.type=='cuda')]
    for opt in optimizers:
        for group in opt.param_groups: group['base_lr']=group['lr']
    def schedule(progress):
        warmup=cfg.get('warmup',0.05)
        if progress<warmup: return max(progress,1e-4)/warmup
        p=min(1,(progress-warmup)/(1-warmup))
        floor=cfg.get('lr_floor',0.1)
        return floor+(1-floor)*0.5*(1+math.cos(math.pi*p))
    loss_fn=token_loss if cfg.get('compile_loss',True) else lambda logits,targets:F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1),targets.flatten())
    model.train(); update=0
    while not budget.exhausted:
        factor=schedule(budget.fraction_used)
        for opt in optimizers:
            for group in opt.param_groups: group['lr']=group['base_lr']*factor
        total=0.0
        for _ in range(cfg['accumulation']):
            batch=ctx.data.sample(cfg['micro_batch'])
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type,dtype=torch.bfloat16): logits=model(batch.tokens)
            loss=loss_fn(logits,batch.targets)
            (loss/cfg['accumulation']).backward()
            total+=loss.detach().item()/cfg['accumulation']
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,cfg.get('clip',1.0))
        for opt in optimizers:
            opt.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update+=1
        if not math.isfinite(total): raise FloatingPointError(f'loss {total} at update {update}')
        if update%cfg.get('log_every',250)==0: ctx.log(dict(update=update,loss=total,lr=optimizers[-1].param_groups[0]['lr']))

from torch import nn
@dataclass
class ModelConfig:
    vocab_size: int = 8192
    d_model: int = 256
    n_heads: int = 4
    n_layers: int = 4
    mlp_width: int = 896
    norm: str = "layer"
    activation: str = "gelu"
    qk_norm: bool = False
    input_skip: bool = False
    attention_width: int = 0
    repeats: int = 3
    depth_norm: bool = True
    init_scale: float = 1.0
    rope_theta: float = 10_000.0
    layer_norm_eps: float = 1e-5
def norm_layer(cfg):
    return nn.RMSNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps) if cfg.norm == "rms" else nn.LayerNorm(cfg.d_model, eps=cfg.layer_norm_eps)
class Attention(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.qk_norm = cfg.qk_norm
        self.n_heads = cfg.n_heads
        self.attention_width = cfg.attention_width or cfg.d_model
        self.head_dim = self.attention_width // cfg.n_heads
        self.c_attn = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, 3 * self.attention_width)
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(self.attention_width, cfg.d_model)
    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):
        batch, length, width = x.shape
        q, k, v = self.c_attn(x).split(self.attention_width, dim=2)
        q, k, v = (t.view(batch, length, self.n_heads, self.head_dim).transpose(1, 2) for t in (q, k, v))
        if self.qk_norm:
            q = F.rms_norm(q, (self.head_dim,))
            k = F.rms_norm(k, (self.head_dim,))
        q, k = q * cos + _rotate_half(q) * sin, k * cos + _rotate_half(k) * sin
        out = F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)
        return self.c_proj(out.transpose(1, 2).reshape(batch, length, self.attention_width))
class MLP(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.activation = cfg.activation
        self.c_fc = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.mlp_width * (2 if cfg.activation == "swiglu" else 1))
        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.mlp_width, cfg.d_model)
    def forward(self, x):
        h = self.c_fc(x)
        if self.activation == "swiglu":
            gate, value = h.chunk(2, dim=-1)
            h = F.silu(gate) * value
        elif self.activation == "relu2":
            h = F.relu(h).square()
        else:
            h = F.gelu(h, approximate="tanh")
        return self.c_proj(h)
class Block(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.ln_1 = norm_layer(cfg)
        self.attn = Attention(cfg)
        self.ln_2 = norm_layer(cfg)
        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)
    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, norms=None):
        ln_1, ln_2 = (self.ln_1, self.ln_2) if norms is None else norms
        x = x + self.attn(ln_1(x), cos, sin)
        return x + self.mlp(ln_2(x))
def _rotate_half(x):
    first, second = x.chunk(2, dim=-1)
    return torch.cat((-second, first), dim=-1)
class GPT(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        self.depth_norms = nn.ModuleList(nn.ModuleList([norm_layer(cfg), norm_layer(cfg)]) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats)) if cfg.depth_norm else None
        self.ln_f = norm_layer(cfg)
        head_dim = (cfg.attention_width or cfg.d_model) // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.blocks.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats) / cfg.init_scale)
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats) / cfg.init_scale)
        if cfg.input_skip:
            self.skip = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats, cfg.d_model))
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version
    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):
        h0 = h
        for r in range(self.cfg.repeats):
            for i, block in enumerate(self.blocks):
                norms = self.depth_norms[r * self.cfg.n_layers + i] if self.depth_norms is not None else None
                if self.cfg.input_skip:
                    h = h + self.skip[r * self.cfg.n_layers + i] * h0
                h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)
        return h
    def forward(self, input_ids):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin())
        return self.ln_f(h)  # the harness applies the tied output head
def build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""
    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))

from pathlib import Path

from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
s=Path('experiments/model_partial.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('    shared_mlp: int = 0','    memory_mlp: bool = False\n    shared_mlp: int = 0')
s=s.replace('class Block(nn.Module):','class MemoryMLP(nn.Module):\n    def __init__(self, cfg):\n        super().__init__()\n        self.gated = cfg.activation == "swiglu"\n        self.query = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model * (2 if self.gated else 1))\n        self.c_proj = nn.Linear(cfg.d_model, cfg.d_model)\n    def forward(self, x, table):\n        q = self.query(x)\n        if self.gated:\n            q1, q2 = q.chunk(2,dim=-1)\n            h = F.silu(F.linear(q1, table)) * F.linear(q2, table)\n        else:\n            h = F.gelu(F.linear(q, table), approximate="tanh")\n        return self.c_proj(F.linear(h, table.T))\n\n\nclass Block(nn.Module):')
s=s.replace('        self.mlp = MLP(cfg)', '        self.memory_mlp = cfg.memory_mlp\n        self.mlp = MemoryMLP(cfg) if cfg.memory_mlp else MLP(cfg)')
s=s.replace('    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, norms=None):','    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, norms=None, table=None):')
s=s.replace('        return x + self.mlp(ln_2(x))','        return x + (self.mlp(ln_2(x), table) if self.memory_mlp else self.mlp(ln_2(x)))')
s=s.replace('h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)','h = block(h, cos, sin, norms, self.embed.weight)')
Path('experiments/model_memory.py').write_text(s)
for name,modelspec in [
 ('memory_gelu',dict(d_model=512,n_layers=4,attention_width=288,n_heads=6,mlp_width=896,repeats=1,shared_mlp=1,memory_mlp=True)),

"""Local research driver; never included in the submission."""
import os,subprocess,sys,time,json
from pathlib import Path
for spec in sys.argv[1:]:
    parts=spec.split(':'); name=parts[0]; seed=int(parts[1]) if len(parts)>1 else 0
    run=Path('runs')/f'{name}_s{seed}'
    if (run/'eval.json').exists():
        print('Already complete',run,flush=True);continue
    print('Starting',run,flush=True)
    with open(str(run)+'.out','w') as out:
        result=subprocess.run([sys.executable,'-u','-m','lmenv.run','train','--submission',f'experiments/{name}','--out',str(run),'--seed',str(seed)],stdout=out,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    if result.returncode:print('Failed',run,flush=True)
    else:print(run,json.loads((run/'eval.json').read_text()),flush=True)

from pathlib import Path
import json,re
print(f'{"Run":28} {"Validation":>10} {"M FLOPs":>8} {"Tokens M":>8} {"Train loss":>10}')
for p in sorted(Path('runs').glob('*_s*')):
    if not p.is_dir():continue
    tr=json.loads((p/'train.json').read_text()) if (p/'train.json').exists() else {}
    ev=json.loads((p/'eval.json').read_text()) if (p/'eval.json').exists() else {}
    out=Path(str(p)+'.out').read_text() if Path(str(p)+'.out').exists() else ''
    matches=re.findall(r'update (\d+)  loss (\S+).*?tokens (\S+)',out)
    last=matches[-1] if matches else None
    cost=tr.get('caps',{}).get('inference_flops_per_token',0)/1e6
    costtext=f'{cost:.1f}' if cost else '-'
    tokens=tr.get('used_tokens',float(last[2]) if last else 0)/1e6
    loss=ev.get('loss'); val=f'{loss:.5f}' if loss else tr.get('status','running')
    print(f'{p.name:28} {val:>10} {costtext:>8} {tokens:8.1f} {last[1] if last else "-":>10}')

def __init__(self, cfg: ModelConfig):
        super().__init__()
        self.cfg = cfg
        self.embed = nn.Embedding(cfg.vocab_size, cfg.d_model)
        self.blocks = nn.ModuleList(Block(cfg) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers))
        if cfg.shared_mlp:
            for i in range(cfg.shared_mlp, cfg.n_layers):
                self.blocks[i].mlp = self.blocks[i % cfg.shared_mlp].mlp
        if cfg.shared_attn:
            for i in range(cfg.shared_attn, cfg.n_layers):
                self.blocks[i].attn = self.blocks[i % cfg.shared_attn].attn
        self.depth_norms = nn.ModuleList(nn.ModuleList([norm_layer(cfg), norm_layer(cfg)]) for _ in range(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats)) if cfg.depth_norm else None
        self.ln_f = norm_layer(cfg)
        head_dim = (cfg.attention_width or cfg.d_model) // cfg.n_heads
        inv_freq = 1.0 / cfg.rope_theta ** (torch.arange(0, head_dim, 2, dtype=torch.float32) / head_dim)
        self.register_buffer("inv_freq", inv_freq, persistent=False)
        nn.init.normal_(self.embed.weight, std=0.02)
        for module in self.blocks.modules():
            if isinstance(module, nn.Linear):
                nn.init.normal_(module.weight, std=0.02)
                nn.init.zeros_(module.bias)
        for block in self.blocks:
            nn.init.normal_(block.attn.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats) / cfg.init_scale)
            nn.init.normal_(block.mlp.c_proj.weight, std=0.02 / math.sqrt(2 * cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats) / cfg.init_scale)
        if cfg.input_skip:
            self.skip = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats, cfg.d_model))
        self.stack = self.apply_blocks  # train.py replaces this with a compiled version
    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin, loops=None, auxiliary=False):
        h0 = h
        saved = []
        loops = self.cfg.repeats if loops is None else loops
        for r in range(loops):
            for i, block in enumerate(self.blocks):
                norms = self.depth_norms[r * self.cfg.n_layers + i] if self.depth_norms is not None else None
                if self.cfg.input_skip:
                    h = h + self.skip[r * self.cfg.n_layers + i] * h0
                h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)
            if auxiliary and r + 1 == max(1, loops // 2):
                saved.append(h)
        return torch.stack(saved + [h], dim=1) if auxiliary else h
    def forward(self, input_ids, loops=None, auxiliary=False):
        length = input_ids.shape[1]
        positions = torch.arange(length, device=input_ids.device, dtype=torch.float32)
        angles = torch.outer(positions, self.inv_freq)
        angles = torch.cat((angles, angles), dim=-1)[None, None]
        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin(), loops, auxiliary)
        return self.ln_f(h)  # the harness applies the tied output head
def build_model(config: dict) -> GPT:
    """config is config.json plus {"vocab_size": ...} from the harness."""
    return GPT(ModelConfig(vocab_size=config["vocab_size"], **config.get("model", {})))
        model.inner.body.stack=torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params=list(model.parameters())
    if cfg.get('optimizer','adamw')=='muon':
        matrix=[]; other=[]
        for name,p in model.named_parameters():
            (matrix if p.ndim==2 and 'embed' not in name else other).append(p)
        optimizers=[Muon(matrix, cfg.get('muon_lr',0.02),cfg.get('muon_decay',0.1)),torch.optim.AdamW(other,lr=cfg['learning_rate'],betas=(0.9,cfg.get('beta2',0.95)),weight_decay=cfg.get('embed_decay',0.1),fused=True)]
    else:
        groups=[{'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if p.dim()>=2 and 'embed' not in n], 'weight_decay':cfg['weight_decay']},
                {'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if 'embed' in n], 'weight_decay':cfg.get('embed_decay',cfg['weight_decay']), 'lr':cfg['learning_rate']*cfg.get('embed_lr_mult',1.0)},
                {'params':[p for p in params if p.dim()<2], 'weight_decay':0.0}]
        optimizers=[torch.optim.AdamW(groups,lr=cfg['learning_rate'],betas=(0.9,cfg.get('beta2',0.95)),fused=ctx.device.type=='cuda')]
    for opt in optimizers:
        for group in opt.param_groups: group['base_lr']=group['lr']
    def schedule(progress):
        warmup=cfg.get('warmup',0.05)
        if progress<warmup: return max(progress,1e-4)/warmup
        p=min(1,(progress-warmup)/(1-warmup))
        floor=cfg.get('lr_floor',0.1)
        return floor+(1-floor)*0.5*(1+math.cos(math.pi*p))
    loss_fn=token_loss if cfg.get('compile_loss',True) else lambda logits,targets:F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1),targets.flatten())
    model.train(); update=0
    while not budget.exhausted:
        factor=schedule(budget.fraction_used)
        for opt in optimizers:
            for group in opt.param_groups: group['lr']=group['base_lr']*factor
        total=0.0
        for _ in range(cfg['accumulation']):
            batch=ctx.data.sample(cfg['micro_batch'])
            options = {}
            progress = budget.fraction_used
            if cfg.get("curriculum",False):
                options["loops"] = 2 if progress < 0.1 else 4 if progress < 0.3 else ctx.config["model"]["repeats"]
            if cfg.get("auxiliary",False):
                options["auxiliary"] = True
            with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type,dtype=torch.bfloat16): logits=model(batch.tokens,**options)
            if cfg.get("auxiliary",False):
                losses = auxiliary_loss(logits,batch.targets)
                weight=cfg.get("aux_weight",0.25)*(1-progress)
                loss=losses[0]*weight+losses[-1]*(1-weight)
            else:
                loss=loss_fn(logits,batch.targets)
            (loss/cfg['accumulation']).backward()
            total+=loss.detach().item()/cfg['accumulation']
        torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,cfg.get('clip',1.0))
        for opt in optimizers:
            opt.step(); opt.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
        update+=1
        if not math.isfinite(total): raise FloatingPointError(f'loss {total} at update {update}')
        if update%cfg.get('log_every',250)==0: ctx.log(dict(update=update,loss=total,lr=optimizers[-1].param_groups[0]['lr']))

from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
s=Path('experiments/model_partial.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('    shared_mlp: int = 0','    depth_gates: bool = False\n    token_shift: bool = False\n    shared_mlp: int = 0')
s=s.replace('    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):','    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, value_gate=None):')
s=s.replace('        if self.qk_norm:', '        if value_gate is not None:\n            v = v * value_gate.view(1, self.n_heads, 1, self.head_dim)\n        if self.qk_norm:')

#64 execcat > experiments/adaptive_queue.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/adaptive_queue.py <<'PY'
"""Research-only worker: claim experiments from an editable local plan."""
import os,time,json,fcntl,subprocess,sys
from pathlib import Path
wait_for=os.environ.get('WAIT_RUN')
while wait_for and not (Path('runs')/wait_for/'eval.json').exists():time.sleep(2)
plan=Path('experiments/plan.json')
while True:
    with plan.open('r+') as f:
        fcntl.flock(f,fcntl.LOCK_EX)
        todo=json.load(f)
        if not todo:break
        item=todo.pop(0);f.seek(0);json.dump(todo,f,indent=2);f.truncate()
    name=item['name'];seed=item.get('seed',0);run=Path('runs')/f'{name}_s{seed}'
    if (run/'eval.json').exists():continue
    args=[sys.executable,'-u','-m','lmenv.run','train','--submission',f'experiments/{name}','--out',str(run),'--seed',str(seed)]
    if 'tokens' in item:args+=['--tokens',str(item['tokens'])]
    print('Starting',run,flush=True)
    with open(str(run)+'.out','w') as out:result=subprocess.run(args,stdout=out,stderr=subprocess.STDOUT)
    if result.returncode:print('Failed',run,flush=True)
    else:print(run,json.loads((run/'eval.json').read_text()),flush=True)

cat > experiments/RESEARCH.md <<'MD'
# Research state

Goal: minimize held-out NLL under 3M non-embedding weights, 120M forward FLOPs/token, 250M training tokens. No internet, external models, extra data or user questions. All submission training forwards use ctx.model. Model always uses tied embedding output from harness. No agents used.

## Current saved method

submission/ currently equals experiments/wide512r4: width 512, two transformer blocks (attention width 256, 4x64 heads, GELU FFN width 896), repeated 4 times with per-depth LayerNorm. Counted weights 2,910,496, FLOPs 35.65M. AdamW original recipe. Root snapshots/002 preserves it with run evidence. Seed0 loss 3.57043 vs our baseline 3.64055 and reference 3.64378. Root snapshots/001 is initial repeat3 experiment.

## Completed results and lessons

Run folders contain eval.json, train.json, model.pt; logs are runs/NAME.out. Run `python experiments/report.py` for latest results. All full trials use 250M tokens and seed 0 unless named otherwise.

- baseline 3.64055 (matches supplied reference).
- repeat3, width256 four blocks repeated3: 3.60151.
- wide384r4, width384 two full-attention blocks, FFN1152 repeated4: 3.56704.
- wide512r4: 3.57043.
- wide512_adamfast (LR .004, warmup .02, embedding decay0): 3.56328. Small gain needs replication.
- wide512_modern (RMSNorm, SwiGLU576, QK norm): 3.56549. No clear gain.
- baseline_muon .02:3.65840. wide512_muon .04:3.64018. wide512_muon08 .08:3.67481. Drop Muon branch.
- wide512_ffnshare: eight independent narrow attention layers, one shared FFN:3.63600.
- wide512_attnshare: one shared attention, eight independent FFN288:3.61055. Independent FFN is more useful than independent attention but full-block repeats beat both.
- wide512r16 near cap (32 effective blocks, 117.44M, 2.9596M weights) currently ahead of r4 by ~.02 on matching mid-late training batches.

## Running fixed experiment queues

Long commands must run foreground in exec_command with yield_time_ms=1000. Plain nohup background processes were killed at shell exit; use persistent sessions.

Five fixed driver sessions, each runs serial jobs:
- 36766: wide512_muon -> wide512_ffnshare -> memory_swiglu.
- 50451: wide512_modern -> wide512_attnshare -> memory_gelu.
- 57646: wide512_adamfast -> wide512r8 -> repeat6.
- 19272: wide512r16 -> wide384r12 -> wide320r4 -> wide512x3r4.
- 45860: wide512_muon08 -> wide512_batch32 -> wide512_batch16 -> wide512r4 seed1 -> wide384r4 seed1.

All jobs use OMP/MKL threads3. Five jobs may run concurrently; GPU ample memory. Smaller batch candidates adjust learning rate and decay: batch32 LR.003 WD.06667, batch16 LR.002 WD.05; accumulation1, warmup.02, embedding decay0.

After fixed queues finish, adaptive workers wait for the last eval.json then claim from shared editable experiments/plan.json with file locks:
- 26310 waits memory_swiglu_s0.
- 73265 waits memory_gelu_s0.
- 76646 waits repeat6_s0.
- 25929 waits wide512x3r4_s0.
- 34821 waits wide384r4_s1.

Change plan under fcntl.flock, to prioritize useful tests / best-method replications. Already running fixed jobs won't read changed code/config after start; do not change their files. Budget 4h total, check bash timer.sh. At last edit ~3h24 remaining.

## Prepared later tests

Plan contains:
- wide512_ffn4: shared attention256; four distinct GELU FFN592 repeated4 =>16 effective blocks, ~53M FLOPs, ~2.997M weights. Inspired by independent FFN ablation.
- wide512r8_gates: 16 effective blocks with depth-specific FFN and value gates, trainable depth mixing (DenseFormer style), learned causal token shift before FFN.
- latent768: embedding512, internal width768, learned in/out bridges, one block attention256 FFN880 repeated20, 2.9938M weights /105.97M FLOPs. Checked.
- wide512r16_curriculum: default32 blocks in eval, training loops2 first10%,4 through30%,16 remainder. It has auxiliary=False. Always output3D. (An earlier auxiliary 4D-output idea was removed from plan to keep strict interface.)
- wide512r16_gates: same gates/depth mixing/token shift at32 blocks, 2,998,096 weights,117.44M FLOPs. Checked.
- wide512_shift: RMS/SwiGLU/QKnorm8-block model plus causal token shift.

Memory models reuse the exempt embedding E inside FFN: Pout E^T GELU(E Pin x), or gated version with two learned queries. Unique weights counted normally; all operations inside model, extra FLOPs counted. Four attention288 blocks GELU (~86.8M) or three attention320 blocks SwiGLU (~94.5M). All checked causal/deterministic/caps.

Other prepared code:
- experiments/train_recipes.py supports EMA averaging (local optimizer state, copied in finally at training end) and WSD schedule. Not yet trialed. Candidate final tests: zero LR floor, EMA .995 from50%, WSD cooldown from70%. Choose winning architecture first.
- latent1024 config exists but not queued.
- model_aux.py/train_aux.py exist; curriculum uses auxiliary=False. No auxiliary-output experiment is queued.

## Next decisions

Use final evals, not training noise, to select. Save snapshots for clear improvements. Need replicate the actual winner at seeds1,2; small gaps ~.01 are seed noise. Consider tuning best architecture batch/LR/EMA/final LR schedule. Prefer final robust gain across seeds, clean submission down to needed model/train/config and optional concise README. Final check should include trained-model checks with recorded calls (using external research helper importing lmenv.checks), in addition to CLI check. Keep all training computation inside ctx.model and no validation/network/filesystem access in submission.
MD

#72
40m

The first memory variants are learning slowly. One hypothesis is that using the same dictionary rows on both sides of GELU leaves the feed-forward operation too close to a linear map. I’ll test a fixed row permutation on the output side; it adds no learned weights, and all computation remains metered.

s=s.replace('        self.gated = cfg.activation == "swiglu"', '        self.gated = cfg.activation == "swiglu"\n        self.register_buffer("permutation", (torch.arange(cfg.vocab_size) * 4053 + 1637) % cfg.vocab_size, persistent=False)')

from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil,fcntl
s=Path('experiments/model_template.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('    attention_width: int = 0','    channel_shuffle: bool = False\n    attention_width: int = 0')
s=s.replace('        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm', '        if cfg.channel_shuffle:\n            indices = torch.arange(cfg.d_model)\n            orders = []\n            for depth in range(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats):\n                multiplier = 4 * depth + 1\n                while math.gcd(multiplier, cfg.d_model) != 1:\n                    multiplier += 2\n                orders.append((indices * multiplier + 97 * depth) % cfg.d_model)\n            order = torch.stack(orders)\n            self.register_buffer("channel_order", order, persistent=False)\n            self.register_buffer("inverse_order", order.argsort(dim=-1), persistent=False)\n        self.ln_f = nn.LayerNorm')
s=s.replace('                h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)', '                depth = r * self.cfg.n_layers + i\n                if self.cfg.channel_shuffle:\n                    h = h.index_select(-1, self.channel_order[depth])\n                h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)\n                if self.cfg.channel_shuffle:\n                    h = h.index_select(-1, self.inverse_order[depth])')
Path('experiments/model_shuffle.py').write_text(s)
d=Path('experiments/wide512r8_shuffle');d.mkdir(exist_ok=True);(d/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy2('experiments/train_template.py',d/'train.py')
c=json.loads(Path('experiments/wide512r8/config.json').read_text());c['model']['channel_shuffle']=True;(d/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2))

I’m also testing whether blocking attention across end-of-document markers improves evaluation of the trained model. If it helps, I’ll validate it as a training method. The mask is causal, deterministic, and adds no learned weights.

Ran 2 commandsexec ×2 (1m)
#89 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
s=Path('experiments/model_template.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('    attention_width: int = 0','    document_mask: bool = True\n    training_document_mask: bool = False\n    attention_width: int = 0')
s=s.replace('    def forward(self, x, cos, sin):','    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, mask=None):')
s=s.replace('F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, is_causal=True)', 'F.scaled_dot_product_attention(q, k, v, attn_mask=mask, is_causal=mask is None)')
s=s.replace('    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, norms=None):', '    def forward(self, x, cos, sin, norms=None, mask=None):')
s=s.replace('self.attn(ln_1(x), cos, sin)', 'self.attn(ln_1(x), cos, sin, mask)')
s=s.replace('    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin):', '    def apply_blocks(self, h, cos, sin, mask=None):')
s=s.replace('h = block(h, cos, sin, norms)', 'h = block(h, cos, sin, norms, mask)')
s=s.replace('        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin())', '        mask = None\n        if self.cfg.document_mask and (not self.training or self.cfg.training_document_mask):\n            documents = (input_ids == 0).cumsum(dim=-1)\n            mask = (documents[:, None, :, None] == documents[:, None, None, :])\n            mask = mask & torch.ones(length, length, dtype=torch.bool, device=input_ids.device).tril()\n        h = self.stack(self.embed(input_ids), angles.cos(), angles.sin(), mask)')
Path('experiments/model_docmask.py').write_text(s)
d=Path('experiments/wide512r8_docmask');d.mkdir(exist_ok=True);(d/'model.py').write_text(s);shutil.copy2('experiments/train_template.py',d/'train.py');shutil.copy2('experiments/wide512r8/config.json',d/'config.json')
r=Path('runs/docmask_eval_s0');r.mkdir(exist_ok=True);shutil.copy2('runs/wide512r8_s0/model.pt',r/'model.pt')
(r/'notes.txt').write_text('Evaluation-only structural masking test on wide512r8_s0 weights. Not a new training run. Default proposed method trains without document masking and enables it in eval.\n')
PY

s=s.replace('self.depth_norms[r * self.cfg.n_layers + i]', 'self.depth_norms[min(r, self.cfg.repeats - 1) * self.cfg.n_layers + i]')

cat > experiments/report.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,re,statistics

def read(p):
 try:return json.loads(p.read_text())
 except (OSError,json.JSONDecodeError):return {}

groups={};active=[];errors=[]
for p in sorted(Path('runs').glob('*_s*')):
 if not p.is_dir() or not re.fullmatch(r'.*_s\d+',p.name):continue
 tr=read(p/'train.json');ev=read(p/'eval.json')
 try:out=Path(str(p)+'.out').read_text()
 except OSError:out=''
 matches=re.findall(r'update (\d+)  loss (\S+).*?tokens (\S+)',out)
 last=matches[-1] if matches else None
 if ev and tr.get('used_tokens',0)>249e6:
  name=re.sub(r'_s\d+$','',p.name)
  groups.setdefault(name,[]).append((tr['seed'],ev['loss'],tr['caps']['inference_flops_per_token']/1e6))
 elif tr.get('status')=='error':errors.append((p.name,tr.get('error')))
 elif not ev and (out or tr):
  tokens=tr.get('used_tokens',float(last[2]) if last else 0)/1e6
  active.append((p.name,tokens,last[1] if last else '-',tr.get('status','running')))
print('BEST FULL METHODS (mean across completed seeds)')
for name,rows in sorted(groups.items(),key=lambda x:statistics.mean(r[1] for r in x[1]))[:10]:
 vals=[r[1] for r in sorted(rows)]
 print(f'{name:27} n={len(vals)} mean={statistics.mean(vals):.5f}  FLOPs={rows[0][2]:.1f}M  seeds='+','.join(f'{x:.5f}' for x in vals))
print('ACTIVE')
for name,tokens,loss,status in active:print(f'{name:30} {tokens:6.1f}M tokens  loss={loss}  {status}')
for name,error in errors:print('ERROR',name,error[:180])
plan=read(Path('experiments/plan.json'))
if isinstance(plan,list):print('Pending adaptive experiments:',len(plan))
PY

from pathlib import Path
import json
p=Path('experiments/model_gates.py');s=p.read_text()
s=s.replace('    token_shift: bool = False','    token_shift: bool = False\n    token_shift_depth: bool = False')

s=s.replace('h = h + self.mix * (previous - h)','h = h + mix * (previous - h)')
lines=s.splitlines(); idx=next(i for i,line in enumerate(lines) if line.startswith('        self.depth_gates = nn.ModuleList'))
lines[idx:idx+1]=['        self.depth_gates = None','        if cfg.depth_gates:','            self.depth_gates = nn.ModuleList()','            for _ in range(cfg.n_layers * cfg.repeats):','                gates = [nn.Parameter(torch.ones(cfg.mlp_width)),','                         nn.Parameter(torch.ones(cfg.attention_width or cfg.d_model)),','                         nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(cfg.mlp_width * (2 if cfg.activation == "swiglu" else 1)))]','                if cfg.token_shift_depth:','                    gates.append(nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(cfg.d_model)))','                self.depth_gates.append(nn.ParameterList(gates))']
s='\n'.join(lines)+'\n';compile(s,'gates','exec');p.write_text(s)
for name in ['wide512r8_gates','wide512r16_gates','wide512_single16_gates','wide512_shift']:
 (Path('experiments')/name/'model.py').write_text(s)
for name in ['wide512r8_gates','wide512_single16_gates']:
 p=Path('experiments')/name/'config.json';c=json.loads(p.read_text());c['model']['token_shift_depth']=True;p.write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2))
PY

from pathlib import Path
s=Path('experiments/train_recipes.py').read_text()
s=s.replace('import math\n','import math\nfrom pathlib import Path\n')
s=s.replace('    ema=None\n', '''    ema=None
    extra_emas={}
    swa_states={}
    def save_average(label, averages):
        # Research-only optimizer-state export; all files stay beside this trainer.
        state={k.replace("_orig_mod.",""):v.detach().cpu() for k,v in model.inner.state_dict().items()}
        for (name,_),average in zip(model.named_parameters(),averages):
            key=name.removeprefix("inner.").replace("_orig_mod.","")
            state[key]=average.detach().cpu()
        torch.save(state,Path(__file__).resolve().parent/(label+".pt"))
''')
s=s.replace('            update+=1', '''            if cfg.get("ema_sweep",False):
                progress=budget.fraction_used
                if progress>=cfg.get("ema_start",0.5):
                    for decay in cfg.get("sweep_decays",[0.98,0.99,0.998,0.999]):
                        if decay not in extra_emas:
                            extra_emas[decay]=[p.detach().clone() for p in params]
                        else:
                            for p,average in zip(params,extra_emas[decay]):
                                average.lerp_(p.detach(),1-decay)
                for start in cfg.get("swa_starts",[0.8,0.9]):
                    if progress>=start:
                        if start not in swa_states:
                            swa_states[start]=[1,[p.detach().clone() for p in params]]
                        else:
                            swa_states[start][0]+=1
                            count,averages=swa_states[start]
                            for p,average in zip(params,averages):
                                average.lerp_(p.detach(),1/count)
            update+=1''')
s=s.replace('    finally:\n        if ema is not None:', '''    finally:
        if cfg.get("ema_sweep",False):
            save_average("raw",[p.detach() for p in params])
            for decay,averages in extra_emas.items():save_average(f"ema_{decay}",averages)
            for start,(_,averages) in swa_states.items():save_average(f"swa_{start}",averages)
        if ema is not None:''')
compile(s,'sweep','exec');Path('experiments/train_sweep.py').write_text(s)

from pathlib import Path
import json,csv,re
reference=json.loads(Path('data/lm/reference.json').read_text())['loss']
rows=[]
for run in Path('runs').iterdir():
 if not run.is_dir() or not (run/'eval.json').exists() or not (run/'train.json').exists():continue
 tr=json.loads((run/'train.json').read_text());ev=json.loads((run/'eval.json').read_text())
 if tr.get('used_tokens',0)<249e6:continue

 conf=Path('experiments')/name/'config.json'
 cfg=json.loads(conf.read_text()) if conf.exists() else {}
 rows.append(dict(method=name,seed=tr['seed'],loss=ev['loss'],gain_vs_reference=reference-ev['loss'],bits_per_byte=ev.get('bits_per_byte'),counted_weights=tr['caps']['weights']['counted'],inference_flops_per_token=tr['caps']['inference_flops_per_token'],used_tokens=tr['used_tokens'],used_flops=tr['used_flops'],train_seconds=tr['train_seconds'],model_config=json.dumps(cfg.get('model',{}),sort_keys=True),train_config=json.dumps(cfg.get('train',{}),sort_keys=True)))
rows.sort(key=lambda r:(r['method'],r['seed']))

  writer=csv.DictWriter(f,fieldnames=list(rows[0]));writer.writeheader();writer.writerows(rows)
