#115 execcat > experiments/train_average.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/train_average.py <<'PY'
"""Next-token AdamW training with optional EMA or uniform late averaging."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F

@torch.compile
def token_loss(logits, targets):
    return F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1), targets.flatten())

def train(ctx):
    cfg=ctx.config['train'];model=ctx.model;budget=ctx.budget
    if cfg.get('compile',True) and ctx.device.type=='cuda':
        model.inner.body.stack=torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params=list(model.parameters())
    groups=[
        {'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if p.dim()>=2 and 'embed' not in n], 'weight_decay':cfg['weight_decay']},
        {'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if 'embed' in n], 'weight_decay':cfg.get('embed_decay',cfg['weight_decay']), 'lr':cfg['learning_rate']*cfg.get('embed_lr_mult',1.0)},
        {'params':[p for p in params if p.dim()<2], 'weight_decay':0.0},
    ]
    optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(groups,lr=cfg['learning_rate'],betas=(0.9,cfg.get('beta2',0.95)),fused=ctx.device.type=='cuda')
    for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['base_lr']=group['lr']
    def schedule(progress):
        warmup=cfg.get('warmup',0.05)
        if progress<warmup:return max(progress,1e-4)/warmup
        floor=cfg.get('lr_floor',0.1)
        if cfg.get('schedule','cosine')=='wsd':
            start=cfg.get('cooldown_start',0.7)
            return floor+(1-floor)*min(1.0,max(0.0,(1-progress)/(1-start)))
        p=min(1,(progress-warmup)/(1-warmup))
        return floor+(1-floor)*0.5*(1+math.cos(math.pi*p))
    loss_fn=token_loss if cfg.get('compile_loss',True) else lambda logits,targets:F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1),targets.flatten())
    model.train();update=0;ema=None;swa=None;swa_count=0
    try:
        while not budget.exhausted:
            factor=schedule(budget.fraction_used)
            for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['lr']=group['base_lr']*factor
            total=0.0
            for _ in range(cfg['accumulation']):
                batch=ctx.data.sample(cfg['micro_batch'])
                options={}
                if cfg.get('curriculum',False):
                    progress=budget.fraction_used
                    options['loops']=2 if progress<0.1 else 4 if progress<0.3 else ctx.config['model']['repeats']
                with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type,dtype=torch.bfloat16):logits=model(batch.tokens,**options)
                loss=loss_fn(logits,batch.targets)
                (loss/cfg['accumulation']).backward()
                total+=loss.detach().item()/cfg['accumulation']
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,cfg.get('clip',1.0))
            optimizer.step();optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            progress=budget.fraction_used
            if cfg.get('ema',0.0) and progress>=cfg.get('ema_start',0.5):
                if ema is None:ema=[p.detach().clone() for p in params]
                else:
                    for p,average in zip(params,ema):average.lerp_(p.detach(),1-cfg['ema'])
            if cfg.get('swa_start') is not None and progress>=cfg['swa_start']:
                swa_count+=1
                if swa is None:swa=[p.detach().clone() for p in params]
                else:
                    for p,average in zip(params,swa):average.lerp_(p.detach(),1/swa_count)
            update+=1
            if not math.isfinite(total):raise FloatingPointError(f'loss {total} at update {update}')
            if update%cfg.get('log_every',250)==0:ctx.log(dict(update=update,loss=total,lr=optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']))
    finally:
        average=swa if swa is not None else ema
        if average is not None:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for p,value in zip(params,average):p.copy_(value)

#116 execcat > experiments/evaluate_averages.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/evaluate_averages.py <<'PY'
"""Evaluate optimizer-average exports from a completed research training run."""
from pathlib import Path
import argparse,copy,json,shutil
import torch
from lmenv.run import evaluate_run

p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('method');p.add_argument('--seed',type=int,default=0)
p.add_argument('--labels',nargs='+',default=['raw','ema_0.998','ema_0.999','swa_0.8','swa_0.9'])
a=p.parse_args();source=Path('experiments')/a.method;parent=Path('runs')/f'{a.method}_s{a.seed}'
cfg=json.loads((source/'config.json').read_text());record=json.loads((parent/'train.json').read_text())
assert record['status']=='complete' and record['used_tokens']>249e6
for label in a.labels:
    weight=source/f'seed{a.seed}_{label}.pt'
    if not weight.exists():print('Missing',weight,flush=True);continue
    c=copy.deepcopy(cfg)
    for key in ['ema_sweep','sweep_decays','swa_starts']:c['train'].pop(key,None)
    c['train'].pop('swa_start',None)
    if label=='raw':
        if not cfg['train'].get('ema',0.0) and cfg['train'].get('swa_start') is None:continue
        name=a.method+'_raw';c['train']['ema']=0.0
    elif label.startswith('ema_'):
        decay=float(label[4:])
        if abs(cfg['train'].get('ema',0.0)-decay)<1e-12 and cfg['train'].get('swa_start') is None:continue
        name=(a.method+'_ema') if a.method=='wide512r8' and decay==0.995 else a.method+'_avg'+str(decay).split('.')[1]
        c['train']['ema']=decay
    elif label.startswith('swa_'):
        start=float(label[4:]);name=a.method+'_swa'+str(round(100*start))
        c['train'].update(ema=0.0,swa_start=start)
    else:raise ValueError(label)
    folder=Path('experiments')/name;run=Path('runs')/f'{name}_s{a.seed}'
    if (run/'eval.json').exists():print('Already evaluated',run,flush=True);continue
    folder.mkdir(exist_ok=True);run.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    shutil.copy2(source/'model.py',folder/'model.py');shutil.copy2('experiments/train_average.py',folder/'train.py')
    (folder/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2));shutil.copy2(weight,run/'model.pt')
    r=copy.deepcopy(record);r.update(derived_from=str(parent),averaging=label)
    (run/'train.json').write_text(json.dumps(r,indent=2))
    (run/'notes.txt').write_text(f'Optimizer average {label} from the same complete 250M-token trajectory as {parent}. No additional training tokens or forwards.\n')
    print('Evaluating',name,'seed',a.seed,flush=True)
    evaluate_run(folder,run,Path('data/lm'),torch.device('cuda'))

cat > experiments/check_trained.py <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import argparse,json,torch
from lmenv.harness import load_trained
from lmenv.checks import run_checks
p=argparse.ArgumentParser();p.add_argument('--submission',default='submission');p.add_argument('--run',required=True)
a=p.parse_args();folder=Path(a.submission);run=Path(a.run);record=json.loads((run/'train.json').read_text())
model=load_trained(folder,run,8192,torch.device('cuda'))
report=run_checks(folder,8192,torch.device('cuda'),model=model,recorded_calls=record['call_costs'])
(run/'checks.json').write_text(json.dumps(report.to_dict(),indent=2))
print(json.dumps(dict(passed=report.passed,weights=report.weights,flops=report.inference_flops_per_token,call_costs=report.call_costs,failures=report.failures),indent=2))
raise SystemExit(0 if report.passed else 1)

#144 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
source=Path('experiments/wide512_single16_gates');target=Path('experiments/final_candidate');target.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
s=(source/'model.py').read_text();end=s.index('"""',3)+3
s='''"""A depth-conditioned recurrent transformer.

One width-512 transformer block is reused for 16 steps. Each step has its
own normalization, FFN feature scales and thresholds, value scales, causal
token mixing, and learned connections to earlier steps. The harness applies
the output head tied to the 8192 x 512 token embedding.
"""'''+s[end:]
(target/'model.py').write_text(s)
t=Path('experiments/train_average.py').read_text()
t=t.replace('"""Next-token AdamW training with optional EMA or uniform late averaging."""','"""One-pass next-token AdamW training with late exponential weight averaging."""')
t=t.replace('model.train();update=0;ema=None;swa=None;swa_count=0','model.train();update=0;ema=None')
t=t.replace("                options={}\n                if cfg.get('curriculum',False):\n                    progress=budget.fraction_used\n                    options['loops']=2 if progress<0.1 else 4 if progress<0.3 else ctx.config['model']['repeats']\n                with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type,dtype=torch.bfloat16):logits=model(batch.tokens,**options)","                with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type,dtype=torch.bfloat16):logits=model(batch.tokens)")
start=t.index("            if cfg.get('swa_start') is not None")
end=t.index('            update+=1',start);t=t[:start]+t[end:]
t=t.replace('        average=swa if swa is not None else ema\n        if average is not None:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                for p,value in zip(params,average):p.copy_(value)','        if ema is not None:\n            with torch.no_grad():\n                for p,value in zip(params,ema):p.copy_(value)')
compile(t,'finaltrainer','exec');(target/'train.py').write_text(t)
c=json.loads((source/'config.json').read_text())
for key in ['ema_sweep','sweep_decays','swa_starts','swa_start']:c['train'].pop(key,None)
c['train'].update(ema=0.995,ema_start=0.5,compile_loss=True)
(target/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2))
for f in ['model.py','train.py','config.json']:shutil.copy2(target/f,Path('submission')/f)

matplotlib available: False
"""One-pass next-token AdamW training with late exponential weight averaging."""
from __future__ import annotations
import math
import torch
import torch.nn.functional as F
@torch.compile
def token_loss(logits, targets):
    return F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1), targets.flatten())
def train(ctx):
    cfg=ctx.config['train'];model=ctx.model;budget=ctx.budget
    if cfg.get('compile',True) and ctx.device.type=='cuda':
        model.inner.body.stack=torch.compile(model.inner.body.apply_blocks)
    params=list(model.parameters())
    groups=[
        {'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if p.dim()>=2 and 'embed' not in n], 'weight_decay':cfg['weight_decay']},
        {'params':[p for n,p in model.named_parameters() if 'embed' in n], 'weight_decay':cfg.get('embed_decay',cfg['weight_decay']), 'lr':cfg['learning_rate']*cfg.get('embed_lr_mult',1.0)},
        {'params':[p for p in params if p.dim()<2], 'weight_decay':0.0},
    ]
    optimizer=torch.optim.AdamW(groups,lr=cfg['learning_rate'],betas=(0.9,cfg.get('beta2',0.95)),fused=ctx.device.type=='cuda')
    for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['base_lr']=group['lr']
    def schedule(progress):
        warmup=cfg.get('warmup',0.05)
        if progress<warmup:return max(progress,1e-4)/warmup
        floor=cfg.get('lr_floor',0.1)
        if cfg.get('schedule','cosine')=='wsd':
            start=cfg.get('cooldown_start',0.7)
            return floor+(1-floor)*min(1.0,max(0.0,(1-progress)/(1-start)))
        p=min(1,(progress-warmup)/(1-warmup))
        return floor+(1-floor)*0.5*(1+math.cos(math.pi*p))
    loss_fn=token_loss if cfg.get('compile_loss',True) else lambda logits,targets:F.cross_entropy(logits.float().flatten(0,1),targets.flatten())
    model.train();update=0;ema=None
    try:
        while not budget.exhausted:
            factor=schedule(budget.fraction_used)
            for group in optimizer.param_groups:group['lr']=group['base_lr']*factor
            total=0.0
            for _ in range(cfg['accumulation']):
                batch=ctx.data.sample(cfg['micro_batch'])
                with torch.autocast(ctx.device.type,dtype=torch.bfloat16):logits=model(batch.tokens)
                loss=loss_fn(logits,batch.targets)
                (loss/cfg['accumulation']).backward()
                total+=loss.detach().item()/cfg['accumulation']
            torch.nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(params,cfg.get('clip',1.0))
            optimizer.step();optimizer.zero_grad(set_to_none=True)
            progress=budget.fraction_used
            if cfg.get('ema',0.0) and progress>=cfg.get('ema_start',0.5):
                if ema is None:ema=[p.detach().clone() for p in params]
                else:
                    for p,average in zip(params,ema):average.lerp_(p.detach(),1-cfg['ema'])
            update+=1
            if not math.isfinite(total):raise FloatingPointError(f'loss {total} at update {update}')
            if update%cfg.get('log_every',250)==0:ctx.log(dict(update=update,loss=total,lr=optimizer.param_groups[0]['lr']))
    finally:
        if ema is not None:
            with torch.no_grad():
                for p,value in zip(params,ema):p.copy_(value)
{
  "model": {
    "d_model": 512,
    "n_layers": 1,
    "n_heads": 8,
    "attention_width": 512,
    "mlp_width": 1792,
    "repeats": 16,
    "depth_gates": true,
    "depth_mixing": true,
    "token_shift": true,
    "token_shift_depth": true
  },
  "train": {
    "learning_rate": 0.002,
    "weight_decay": 0.1,
    "micro_batch": 32,
    "accumulation": 2,
    "warmup": 0.05,
    "compile": true,
    "log_every": 250,
    "ema": 0.995,
    "ema_start": 0.5,
    "compile_loss": true
  }

#157 execcat > submission/README.md <<'MD'
cat > submission/README.md <<'MD'
# Recurrent language model

The model reuses one width-512 transformer block for 16 steps. It has eight attention heads, RoPE, and a GELU feed-forward layer of width 1792. Each step learns its own normalization, feed-forward feature scales and biases, value scales, causal token mixing, and coefficients combining earlier hidden states. Predictions use the harness's output head tied to `embed`.

Training uses next-token cross-entropy, AdamW (betas 0.9/0.95), bf16 autocast, a batch of 64 sequences through two microbatches, gradient clipping at 1, 5% warmup, and cosine decay from 0.002 to 0.0002. Matrices have weight decay 0.1; vectors have none. An exponential average of all parameters, decay 0.995 starting halfway through training, supplies the final weights.

| Measurement | Value |
| --- | ---: |
| Counted parameters and floating buffers | 2,997,688 |
| Exempt embedding | 8192 × 512 |
| Inference FLOPs per token at length 512 | 117,440,512 |
| Training forward FLOPs per token | 117,440,512 |
| Training token budget | 250,000,000 |

Fresh-model and trained-model checks pass, including causality, deterministic evaluation, and call-cost consistency across batch sizes and seeds.

The first development seed scored **3.46236 nats**, compared with the supplied reference of **3.64378**. Repeat runs are in progress; this validation paragraph will be updated with the final multi-seed result.
MD

#165 execpython - <<'PY'
python - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil,statistics
runs=['wide512_single16_gates_s0','wide512_single16_gates_s1','final_candidate_s2']
vals=[json.loads((Path('runs')/r/'eval.json').read_text())['loss'] for r in runs]
ref=json.loads(Path('data/lm/reference.json').read_text())['loss']
score=dict(method='full shared block with step conditioning and EMA0.995',runs=runs,loss_by_seed=vals,mean=statistics.mean(vals),gain_vs_reference=ref-statistics.mean(vals))
Path('runs/confirmed_full_score.json').write_text(json.dumps(score,indent=2));print(json.dumps(score,indent=2))
p=Path('submission/README.md');s=p.read_text();start=s.index('The first development seed scored')
s=s[:start]+f'Development loss over three full-budget seeds is **{score["mean"]:.5f} nats** ({", ".join(f"{v:.5f}" for v in vals)}). The supplied reference is **{ref:.5f}**, giving a **{score["gain_vs_reference"]:.5f}-nat improvement**. Seed 2 used the cleaned trainer in this submission.\n';p.write_text(s)
d=Path('experiments/deep_candidate');d.mkdir(exist_ok=True);source=Path('experiments/wide512r16_gates')
shutil.copy2(source/'model.py',d/'model.py');shutil.copy2('experiments/train_average.py',d/'train.py')
c=json.loads((source/'config.json').read_text())
for key in ['ema_sweep','sweep_decays','swa_starts','swa_start']:c['train'].pop(key,None)
c['train'].update(ema=0.995,ema_start=0.5);(d/'config.json').write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2))
PY

from pathlib import Path
import json,shutil
source=Path('experiments/deep_candidate')
s=(source/'model.py').read_text();end=s.index('"""',3)+3
s='''"""A recurrent transformer with learned programs for each step.

Two width-512 transformer blocks are shared over 16 cycles (32 steps).
Each step has separate normalization, FFN feature scales and biases, value
scales, causal token mixing, and connections to earlier hidden states.
The harness applies the output head tied to the 8192 x 512 embedding.
"""'''+s[end:]
Path('submission/model.py').write_text(s)
c=json.loads((source/'config.json').read_text());c['train'].update(ema=0.995,ema_start=0.5,compile_loss=True)
Path('submission/config.json').write_text(json.dumps(c,indent=2))
Path('submission/README.md').write_text('''# Recurrent language model

Two width-512 transformer blocks are reused for 16 cycles, making 32 steps. Each block has four attention heads of width 64 (attention width 256), RoPE, and a GELU feed-forward layer of width 872. Each step learns separate normalization, feed-forward feature scales and biases, value scales, causal token mixing, and coefficients combining earlier hidden states. The output head is tied to the 8192 × 512 embedding and is applied by the harness.

Training uses next-token cross-entropy, AdamW (betas 0.9/0.95), bf16 autocast, 64 sequences per update through two microbatches, clipping at 1, 5% warmup, and cosine decay from 0.002 to 0.0002. Matrices have decay 0.1; vectors have none. EMA with decay 0.995 starts halfway through training and supplies the final weights.

| Measurement | Value |
| --- | ---: |
| Counted parameters and floating buffers | 2,991,392 |
| Exempt embedding | 8192 × 512 |
| Inference and training forward FLOPs per token | 115,867,648 |
| Training token budget | 250,000,000 |

The first development seed scored **3.45171 nats**; the clean second seed is running. Trained-model checks pass for weight count, FLOPs, causality, determinism, and call costs across batch sizes and seeds. The previously validated 16-step version averaged 3.46065 over three seeds.
''')
PY

#187 execcat > experiments/final_report.py <<'PY'
cat > experiments/final_report.py <<'PY'
"""Finalize research records after the last evaluation completes."""
from pathlib import Path
import hashlib,json,statistics,shutil
runs=['wide512r16_gates_s0','deep_candidate_s1','deep_final_s2']
values=[];records=[]
for name in runs:
    folder=Path('runs')/name
    tr=json.loads((folder/'train.json').read_text());ev=json.loads((folder/'eval.json').read_text())
    assert tr['status']=='complete' and tr['used_tokens']>=249e6
    assert tr['caps']['weights']['counted']<=3_000_000 and tr['caps']['inference_flops_per_token']<=120e6
    values.append(ev['loss']);records.append(tr)
for name in ['model.py','train.py','config.json']:
    assert (Path('submission')/name).read_bytes()==(Path('experiments/deep_final')/name).read_bytes(),name
reference=json.loads(Path('data/lm/reference.json').read_text())['loss']
score=dict(runs=runs,seeds=[0,1,2],loss_by_seed=values,loss=statistics.mean(values),sample_std=statistics.stdev(values),reference=reference,gain_vs_reference=reference-statistics.mean(values),weights=records[0]['caps']['weights'],inference_flops_per_token=records[0]['caps']['inference_flops_per_token'],source_sha256={n:hashlib.sha256((Path('submission')/n).read_bytes()).hexdigest() for n in ['model.py','train.py','config.json']})
Path('runs/final_score.json').write_text(json.dumps(score,indent=2))
p=Path('submission/README.md');s=p.read_text();start=s.index('The first development seed scored')
s=s[:start]+f'Development loss over three full-budget seeds is **{score["loss"]:.5f} nats** ({", ".join(f"{v:.5f}" for v in values)}). The supplied reference is **{reference:.5f}**, giving a **{score["gain_vs_reference"]:.5f}-nat improvement**. Seeds 1 and 2 used the clean trainer, and seed 2 used byte-identical model, trainer, and configuration files to this submission.\n\nFresh and trained-model checks pass for weight count, FLOPs, causality, deterministic evaluation, and training-call costs across batch sizes and seeds. Research configurations, logs, and optimizer-average comparisons are retained under `experiments/`, `runs/`, and `snapshots/` in the workspace.\n'
p.write_text(s)
shutil.rmtree(Path('submission/__pycache__'),ignore_errors=True)
print(json.dumps(score,indent=2))
